class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. It establishes that Dacre paid for the accusation.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. It identifies the person who prepared the envelope’s contents.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. It confirms Mara’s legal ownership of the flower.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. Mara moved nothing, but Cassian asked whether she intended to delete the photograph. His voice was so mild that several guests looked up to see why it had become dangerous.","\"Don't be absurd, Cassian. The envelope could contain anything.\"","\"Exactly,\" Mara said. \"The photograph establishes neither a theft nor a payment. You chose one interpretation before asking a question.\"","Lady Ottilie set down her fork. \"That does not explain the name on your invitation.\"","There it was: the other trap. Mara looked towards the banqueting room doors, where the staff had withdrawn to give their employers privacy. Privacy, in this household, apparently included twenty witnesses and excluded anyone who might understand what it cost to be accused.","\"I gave your office my proper name,\" she said. \"Someone added another.\"","\"Arden,\" said the woman. \"A very old connection. An applicant for a restoration scholarship might find it useful.\"","\"And a public figure might find it useful to confuse a missing letter with an ancestry.\"","The woman's face changed. \"Are you suggesting I fabricated it?\"","\"I am suggesting we locate the original invitation.\"","An uneasy silence spread through the room. Mara could cope with anger. This discreet collective absorption in her disgrace was harder. Nobody wanted to appear unfair-minded; few seemed willing to risk an individual opinion before the general disposition became clear.","Elias rose. \"I have the guest record.\"","\"There is no need for a fuss,\" Ottilie said.","\"Then this will be wonderfully brief.\"","He returned with a tablet and an old-fashioned fax. The invitation had been changed through the events office that afternoon. An assistant had received a message which purported to be from Mara. Its signature used her full name, but its return address belonged to an outside communications agency.","\"The agency handles our charity advertising,\" the hostile woman said quickly. \"It is a common clerical blunder.\"","\"It may be,\" Mara replied. \"Then you ought to revise the accusation, rather than ask me to conform to it.\"","The elderly gentleman at the door laughed again. Ottilie gave him a look that should have ended in a farewell. Instead, he introduced himself as Professor Penn, a retired historian from Yorkshire.","\"I would not overrate the innocence of a clerical error,\" he said. \"Nor its malice, without evidence. Let the young woman finish.\"","Mara had expected an emissary of privilege. She had found, at least for the duration of a dinner, a fair-minded arbiter. She took a breath and drew her mother's flower from her pocket.","The silver petals opened beneath the chandelier. Their glitter made the guests lean forward. Now the room's attention belonged to something she could handle.","\"This is the object I examined at the auction. The catalogue exclusively attributes it to an unnamed European pioneer. The account omits my mother's work.\"","\"You are a restorer, not an art historian,\" the woman said.","\"Fortunately, I can tell the difference between a claim and a screw.\"","Mara pointed to a small fitting beneath the flower. \"My mother designed a removable hinge to allow repairs without damage. She used gum from a particular supplier. Her ledger describes its tendency to become sticky in warm conditions. The catalogue calls this fixed construction. It isn't.\"","\"That doesn't preclude another restorer.\"","\"No. This does.\"","She turned the base towards a lamp. A shallow sequence of dots appeared underneath the marquetry. They were not an ornament but a workshop mark, concealed by an accumulation of dirty varnish. Mara had once watched Elena carve them while teaching her to count.","\"There is no title attached to it,\" Mara continued. \"No promise of intermarriage between noble families. No long-lost lord. Only a woman who did the work and a daughter who remembers.\"","Cassian remained beside her without speaking. She was grateful. His support mattered partly because he did not make himself the essence of her defence.","Professor Penn examined the mark through his spectacles. \"I have seen this workshop code. The Museum of Applied Art has an annal of local acquisitions covering that period. It would permit a cross-check.\"","\"Then we can get started tomorrow,\" Mara said.","The hostile woman leaned back. \"A moving story. Stories abound in the restoration business.\"","\"As do invoices,\" Elias replied. \"Unlike stories, they sometimes survive an audit.\"","Ottilie looked from one face to another. At last, with visible reluctance, she asked the woman to withdraw the suggestion that Mara had invented a family name.","\"If my remarks have had an adverse impact—\"","\"They did,\" Mara said.","\"Then I regret the misunderstanding.\"","It was less than an apology. Mara accepted the withdrawal, not the fiction that everyone had misunderstood together. Equality at a dinner table might begin with something as small and fragile as that distinction.","Dessert arrived. The evening tried to become ordinary again. A guest asked Cassian about the absence of a ring. Another suggested that the best-practice approach to an engagement was a formal family announcement. Mara wondered whether she could crawl underneath the linen and leave unnoticed.","Cassian looked at her before he answered.","\"We have made no pledge about a wedding date.\"","\"But you will marry?\" asked Ottilie.","\"We will tell you what we choose to make public.\"","Mara felt a sudden, inappropriate warmth. The answer did not commit her to anything. That was why it meant something.","Then the doors opened, and Selene Ardent entered wearing a plain black blouse and a necklace that resembled a captured star. Mara recognised her from a charity ad displayed at every tram stop. The foundation's record-high contribution to medical recovery had made her unusually difficult to criticise.","\"I hear you have found a family treasure,\" Selene said.","Her manner was gentle. It was also the manner of someone who expected the room to accelerate or cease at her convenience.","\"I found an incomplete account,\" Mara replied.","Selene considered the flower. \"Restoration is a beautiful profession. It brings order to things that have suffered.\"","\"Sometimes the proper task is to remove the order someone imposed on them.\"","\"Of course. A restorer must know when to intervene.\"","She offered Cassian her hand. He shook it, briefly. Mara saw no atom of surprise in Selene's expression, although the rumour of their engagement had evidently travelled faster than their car.","\"I am an optimistic supporter of your work,\" Selene told Mara. \"We are about to phase in a new programme for talented young conservators. There is a broad range of accommodation, training, and mentorship.\"","\"With exclusive ownership of the results?\"","\"Not anymore. We refine our parameters when experience reveals a weakness.\"","It was an intelligible offer. That made it more unsettling. Mara could imagine the relief of a first-class workshop, of a clean floor, of tools that did not require constant toil. She could also imagine how quickly a reasonable contribution might become a chain.","\"You could amass a meaningful body of work,\" Selene said. \"Instead of being prey to the city's appetite for a story.\"","Mara closed the flower. \"I already have work.\"","\"You have a struggle. They are not always the same thing.\"","Before Mara could answer, an attendant brought Selene a phone. She read the screen, excused herself, and moved away with the calm of a worshipper confident that the god would wait.","The room began to breathe again. Mara hated herself a little for noticing.","On the terrace, cold air carried the smell of rain and a guest's cigarette. Mara took off the borrowed necklace. She had retrieved it from the auction locker that morning and meant to return it before its owner could accuse the wrong household.","Cassian stood beside the railing, leaving her space.","\"You didn't enjoy your debut,\" he said.","\"I enjoyed three minutes. I may repeat those in my memoir.\"","\"I particularly liked the screw.\"","\"Obviously. An investigator appreciates a thrilling acquisition history.\"","He smiled. \"You were very good.\"","Compliments from him did not arrive as decoration. She felt them somewhere less defended than vanity.","\"Don't overrate me,\" she said. \"I was afraid.\"","\"Competence doesn't require the absence of fear.\"","She studied the scar on his wrist. \"Does it ever cease?\"","\"Not entirely. Sometimes you have someone to stand beside.\"","For a moment, the space between them felt less like a safeguard and more like an invitation. Mara did not cross it. She opened the flower and removed the recording strip instead.","\"You keep this. I keep the invoice. Two originals in two places.\"","He understood. \"An auxiliary safeguard.\"","\"And a reason to find each other if somebody tries to isolate us.\"","He accepted it with a seriousness that made her wish she had chosen a lighter phrase.","His phone vibrated before he could reply. A message from her landlady appeared on Mara's own screen a second later.","Two officials at your workshop. Asking about stolen goods. I haven't let them in.","Mara's hand closed around the phone.","The dinner had ended in a withdrawal. It had not ended the accusation."],"questions":[{"type":"main_point","stem":"Which development best captures the limit of Mara’s success at the dinner?","options":["Selene’s training offer removes Mara’s need to investigate the invoice.","The guests agree to place the investigation under Ottilie’s control.","The accusation about her name is withdrawn, but a new restriction reaches her workshop.","Her identification of the repair mark ends the dispute over the flower."],"answer":2,"explanation":"本章先撤回冒名暗示，结尾却传来店铺被查的消息。一次公开反击有效，但压力没有结束；其他选项都扩大了当晚结果。","evidence":[83],"shuffled":true}],"wordCount":1496,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0005\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">卷一｜假婚约与失踪证人</div><h1>The Work They Erased</h1><p id=\"chapter-0005-p1\"><a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">The</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-9271\" data-words=\"9271\">woman</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-125\" data-words=\"125\">across</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">the</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8240\" data-words=\"8240\">table</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-6753\" data-words=\"6753\">reached for</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-3891\" data-words=\"3891\">her</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-6139\" data-words=\"6139\">phone</a>. Her landlady, Mrs. Cress, stood beneath a streetlight carrying a saucepan as though she meant to arrest someone with it.","\"I warned them,\" she said.","\"With that?\" Cassian asked.","\"A soup ladle lacks authority.\"","A string of paper seals crossed Mara's window. Behind the glass stood a chest she had repaired, a model ship waiting for paint, and the iron press that had belonged to her mother. The little shop's apparent peace made the notice look unreal.","Cassian examined the document. \"A temporary preservation order. It doesn't authorize removal.\"","\"It deprives her of her tools,\" Mrs. Cress said. \"What do they suppose she eats? Air?\"","Mara looked away. She could accept misfortune more easily when nobody named it in a generous voice.","\"We'll request the underlying accusation,\" Cassian said. \"Tonight, I can make sure the order and inventory match. Tomorrow, an independent lawyer can challenge its scope.\"","\"Do you know an independent lawyer?\"","\"One who dislikes me.\"","\"Ideal,\" Mara said.","He handed her his phone without claiming that his title would solve the problem. She photographed each seal, recorded the time, and asked Mrs. Cress what the inspectors had said explicitly. Their use of a legitimate procedure did not establish that its purpose was legitimate; however, breaking the seals would provide a much simpler charge than the one they appeared to be investigating.","Mara had to remain outside her own door.","She hated how sensible it was.","At sunrise, June brought a reply from Reed's daughter. Her name was Leda. She would meet them at the Lantern Theatre if they came without cameras. \"No social promotion,\" her message added. \"No surprise reunion for a viewer to cry over.\"","Mara liked her immediately.","The theatre's back entrance led through a cellar smelling of hay, paste, and damp wooden scenery. An enormous painted moon leaned against a staircase. On a nearby table, a porcelain rabbit wore a bachelor hat and a beard.","\"Our next production,\" June said. \"A comedy about a banker who tries to wed the wrong widow.\"","\"Surely that is most banking,\" Cassian said.","Leda appeared from behind the moon. She was small, with broad shoulders and a practical braid, and she held a comb between her teeth. A costume assistant followed with a vest.","\"Arms up,\" the assistant said.","Leda obeyed. \"I'm sorry. They scheduled the fitting before I knew my private life would develop into a mystery.\"","\"We can wait,\" Mara said.","\"No, stay. She's putting pins in me. The average witness is more credible while immobilized.\"","The assistant gave a good-natured snort and worked in silence. Leda watched Mara, then Cassian, with an expression that was neither cordial nor hostile. It was the look of someone testing whether civility would be used against her.","Noor showed her a still image from Kingfisher. Leda's lip tightened.","\"It looks like him.\"","\"We can't be sure from the picture,\" Noor said.","\"No. But that is how he holds a broken thing. He turns the sharp edge away from himself, then forgets that the other edge is sharp too.\"","She tried to laugh. The attempt made Mara's throat ache.","\"When did you last see him?\" Cassian asked.","\"A year ago. At a clinic. They said a visit might impair his recovery, so I didn't insist on another. Then the clinic closed and the records went anywhere but to me.\"","\"Did he consent to the restriction?\"","\"They said he had. I never heard him say it.\"","The costume assistant withdrew a pin. Leda stepped down from the fitting platform and wrapped a robe over her shoulders. She looked suddenly very young.","\"I thought I was being helpful,\" she said.","Noor did not tell her that everything was all right. \"You acted on information people with responsibility gave you. Now we can ask for better information.\"","Leda's face changed a little. Not relief, exactly; more like room to breathe.","Cassian played the recording. At the chess phrase she closed her eyes.","\"He used to let me win,\" she said. \"Not by making an obvious mistake. He would build this magnificent trap and then take a step in the wrong direction. I'd be proud for a week.\"","\"What did the eagle mean?\"","\"A knight with a broken head. I glued a bird on top. My father said it was innovational design.\"","She opened her eyes and smiled without happiness. \"He enjoyed the wrong adjective.\"","The distinction mattered. A phrase that had sounded coded might simply be family language. Its presence on the strip supported an association with Reed; it did not prove when he had spoken or why.","\"I want to visit,\" Leda said. \"But I don't want you to turn him into a champion for a case he doesn't understand.\"","\"Nor do we,\" Mara replied.","\"People say that before they need something.\"","Mara thought of the flower and the sealed shop. \"Then let us write down what we may and may not do. You keep a copy. If we disagree later, you have more than a memory of our generosity.\"","Leda gave her a long look. \"You sound like a woman who's met my landlord.\"","A stage manager interrupted with a letter about the restoration grant. The foundation wanted another review of the theatre's finances before releasing the next payment. Leda read it, then passed it to June without comment.","\"Is this because of us?\" Mara asked.","\"It could be,\" June said. \"It could also be a review already scheduled. I can check the dates.\"","\"The carpenter needs paying on Friday,\" Leda said. \"The dates won't alter that.\"","Mara looked at the unfinished scenery. Her victory at dinner had seemed confined to a table. Here, its possible price had a workshop, a payroll, and people who had not chosen to join her struggle.","They made a simple agreement. Noor would request a supported visit through an independent patient advocate. Leda could withdraw from publicity at any stage. If Reed agreed to speak, he could choose the subject and stop when he wished. No recording would be published without a separate decision.","\"What about a reward?\" Leda asked.","\"For information?\" June said.","\"For not making a press conference out of my father.\"","\"I accept payment in tea.\"","Leda handed her a chipped cup. \"We may succeed.\"","While Noor made calls, Mara helped the costume assistant untangle a cascade of ribbon. The work was ordinary enough to make the room feel safer. A damaged hem, unlike a human story, did not object when someone tried to complete it.","Leda showed her an old scrapbook. Its early pages held playbills, oral-history notes, and a postcard from her uncle in Australia. The theatre had once staged a folk comedy by an exiled Briton whose youth had been spent in a Highland town. Several later plays had been written by local workers.","One page carried an old racial description beside a performer's photograph. Leda covered the word with a loose paper slip.","\"The archive keeps it,\" she said. \"The new edition explains why we don't use it to describe him. My father insisted on that. He said remembering a cruelty didn't require repeating it carelessly.\"","Mara nodded. Below the photograph, the performer had written that theatre could make an alien feel at home without requiring him to become someone else. It was a lofty sentiment, but the man's pencil corrections around it were wonderfully human.","At the back, a loose rectangle of paper fell out.","It was a receipt for a typewriter.","\"Dad bought that for me,\" Leda said. \"I wanted to become a writer. I was eleven. He thought the machine would make the stories arrive.\"","\"Did it?\"","\"Mostly it taught me to swear.\"","Mara turned it over. Across the back ran a line of addresses. One was Kingfisher House. Beside it, in different ink, were the words OLD EXCHANGE, SOUTHERN DOOR.","\"Do you know this place?\"","\"A rehearsal warehouse by the river. We used to store props there before the merger of the theatre companies. It had a stage door wider than this room.\"","Cassian looked at the date. \"This was written before the fire.\"","\"The ink could have been added later,\" Mara said.","\"Yes.\"","He accepted the correction instantly. Leda watched them with increasing interest.","\"You two don't behave like newlyweds,\" she observed.","\"We aren't newlyweds,\" Mara said.","\"Nor like an engaged couple.\"","\"What does an engaged couple do?\"","\"Ignore every question in order to talk about themselves. You're vastly more inconvenient.\"","Cassian's mouth moved. Mara decided to concentrate on the receipt.","Noor returned with an appointment. An advocate could visit Kingfisher with Leda at three. Dacre had agreed after learning that the clinic's former administrator had confirmed Reed's transfer into its network. That did not confirm he remained there, but it narrowed the uncertainty.","Leda sat down. \"I'm afraid he'll be disappointed in me.\"","\"Why?\" Mara asked.","\"I let them keep him away.\"","\"You kept looking.\"","\"Not militantly enough.\"","Mara knelt beside the chair, careful of the repaired hem. \"You don't owe anyone a particular performance of love.\"","For the first time, Leda took her hand.","A shout came from the rehearsal room. The model moon had broken loose, rolled through a doorway, and trapped June against a wall. She was laughing too hard to escape.","Cassian went to help. His first attempt merely made the moon sway.","\"A formidable adversary,\" he said.","Leda laughed properly then, a brief, astonished sound.
